All hooks in. Now write tools/ui-shots/shots.mjs. Design details:

- Node 22+, zero deps.

- Browser discovery: env CROSSEXAM_BROWSER, else candidates list:

  Linux: which google-chrome / chromium / chromium-browser / microsoft-edge.
  Use fs.existsSync for absolute ones; for misleading names, try `which`? Keep simple: absolute candidates + env override.

Two issues:

The message "截图失败：EPERM, Permission denied: ...Temp\crossexam-shots-RQutBk" — that's rmSync failing because the browser (Edge) still holds the profile dir (kill is async). - rmWithRetry function.

Implement: 
```
async function removeDirWithRetry(dir, attempts) {
  for (let i = 0; i < attempts; i += 1) {
    try { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); return true; }
    catch { await sleep(400); }
  }
  return false;
}
```
And in finally:
```
if (browser !== null) { browser.kill(); await sleep(500); }
if (server !== null) { server.kill(); }
await sleep(300);
if (!opts.keep) {
  const removed = await removeDirWithRetry(workDir, 5);
  if (!removed) log('临时目录没删干净（浏览器还占着），不影响出图：' + workDir);
}
```

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Real bug in my 07 shot expression: I used `const pane` twice? No — 07 uses `'const pane = ' + panes.resume + '; pane.scrollTop = 90;'` and 06 also declared `const pane`. Since Runtime.evaluate runs in the same global scope, `const pane` persists → redeclaration error on the second evaluate. Right.

Fix: wrap each evaluate in an IIFE, or use `var`. Better: make the scroll spinner a function: set `window.__cxScroll = (sel, top) => ...` once, then call. Simplest: wrap expressions in `(function(){ ... })()`. Let me define a helper in the tool: `scrollExpr(paneKey, ratio)` returns an IIFE.

Let me restructure the shot/scroll helpers:
- `const paneExpr = (name) => "document.querySelector('[data-shot=\"" + name + "\"]').lastElementChild"` — avoid const declarations entirely by using expressions with no declarations:
  - top: `paneExpr(name) + '.scrollTop = 0'` ✓ (no declaration)
  - bottom: `paneExpr(name) + '.scrollTop = ' + paneExpr(name) + '.scrollHeight'` ✓
  - focus click: `paneExpr('claims') + ".querySelector('button.quote').click()"` — but need existence check; use optional chaining: `(paneExpr + ".querySelector('button.quote')?.click(), 1")` — comma expression fine.
  - fractional scroll: `paneExpr + '.scrollTop = ' + paneExpr + '.scrollHeight * 0.55'`

That removes all declarations. Let me apply.

</details>

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

`meta.contracts` stores `{id, version, digest}[]` — so contracts are persisted as metadata, but only id/version/digest, not the full text.

For the UI to show the full 意图契约 (goal/tactics/forbidden/budget), we can reconstruct it deterministically: `buildContracts(panel, budgetView)` from shared — the same function the orchestrator used. The stored digest lets us *verify* the reconstruction matches what actually ran (integrity check!). That's a nice touch: UI shows "契约指纹 abc123 与运行时一致" — 真·意图控制可验证。

Design:
- Server: add `GET /api/contracts?panel=tech,hr,hiring_manager&budget=3,2,60,2`? Simpler: return contracts for the default panel + default budget, plus digests. But a ran session may use a different panel. Better: include contracts in `GET /api/sessions/:id` response? The session meta has contracts [{id,version,digest}] but not panel list... panel is derivable from contracts ids! And budget from session.budget.config.

So: in `assembleSession`, add `contracts` field: rebuild via `buildContracts(panelFromMeta, budgetView(session.budget.config))`, attach stored digest, and verify: `matches: contractDigest(rebuilt) === stored.digest`. 

Is buildContracts available in shared exports? Yes: contract.ts exports buildContracts, contractDigest, renderSystemPrompt, PERSONA_PRESETS etc.

Where does SessionRow store budget? `session.budget: BudgetState` per store docs. Its config is what we need.

Implementation in pipeline.ts:
```ts
import { buildContracts, contractDigest } from '@crossexam/shared';
...
export interface ContractView {
  id: string; version: number; digest: string; verified: boolean; contract: IntentContract;
}
```
and in assembleSession:
```ts
const stored = metaContracts(session.meta);  // [{id,version,digest}]
const panel = stored.map(s => s.id).filter(isInterviewerRole)
const rebuilt = buildContracts(panel, budgetView(session.budget.config))
contracts = rebuilt.map(c => { const s = stored.find(x => x.id === c.id); return { id: c.id, version: c.version, digest: contractDigest(c), storedDigest: s?.digest ?? null, verified: s?.digest === contractDigest(c) ... }})
```
Careful: `buildContracts(roles, budgetView, version = 1)`. Need budgetView — shared exports `ContractBudgetView`; pipeline likely already builds one for the run. Let me check run.ts/pipeline.ts for how it constructs budgetView (maybe `budgetViewOf`). Let me grep.

Then web: `api.ts` add `ContractView` type + field on SessionDetail; and StageRail renders a collapsible 「意图契约」 section per interviewer showing goal/tactics/forbidden/budget + digest + verified mark. Since a session may not be loaded (live run), contracts only come from history fetch... During live run the web doesn't have the contracts. Options: also expose `GET /api/contracts?panel=...` for the live panel (deterministic, no LLM) — then the UI can show contracts even before running (they're declared intent, not results!). That's even more compelling: 契约是在跑之前就写死的。

So implement both:
1. `GET /api/contracts?panel=tech,hr` → `{ budget: ContractBudgetView, contracts: [{...contract, digest}] }` using DEFAULT_BUDGET and given panel (validate roles; empty → default panel).
2. Session detail: contracts with verification against stored digest (shows 契约漂移检测).

Web: fetch contracts when panel changes (or on load) and render inside StageRail under each interviewer: expandable 「意图契约」 showing goal, tactics, forbidden list, budget numbers; header shows digest 指纹 and (for history) 与运行时一致/不一致.

That's a decent chunk of work: server route + view + web component + tests + docs. Maybe 1.5 hours of my steps. Value: makes innovation #3 visible and 可验证 — strong for the 意图控制 20% criterion. I'll do a *focused* version:
- Server: `/api/contracts` route + `contracts` in SessionView with `verified` flag.
- Web: StageRail per-interviewer expandable contract block (client-side state), fetch once on panel change.
- Tests: server test for the route (default panel + custom panel + invalid role), and web test for a small helper (e.g., `contractSummary` rendering lines?). Keep web test minimal: test a pure formatter that turns a contract into display rows (goal/tactics/forbidden/budget lines) — keeps the drift-prone logic tested.

Let me check shared exports for budgetView helper and run.ts usage.

</details>

```ts
export interface ContractView {
  id: string;
  version: number;
  /** 服务端此刻重新算出的指纹 */
  digest: string;
  /** 运行时落库的指纹；未跑过或旧会话可能是 null */
  storedDigest: string | null;
  /** 契约全文，把它算出来的指纹与运行时一致 */
  verified: boolean;

}
```
And `contractViews(roles, budget, stored)`.

Where does `stored` come from: `session.meta.contracts`. Types: Array<{id?: unknown; version?: unknown; digest?: unknown}>.

Implementation:
```ts
interface StoredContract { id: string; version: number; digest: string }

function metaContracts(meta: Record<string, unknown>): StoredContract[] {
  const value = meta['contracts'];
  if (!Array.isArray(value)) return [];
  const out: StoredContract[] = [];
  for (const item of value) {
    if (item === null || typeof item !== 'object') continue;
    const raw = item as Record<string, unknown>;
    const id = raw['id'];
    const digest = raw['digest'];
    const version = raw['version'];
    if (typeof id !== 'string' || typeof digest !== 'string') continue;
    out.push({ id, version: typeof version === 'number' ? version : 1, digest });
  }
  return out;
}

/** 契约是声明式意图，不在表里存全文：这里按同样的输入重新算一遍，并和运行时落下的指纹对账。 */
export function contractViews(
  roles: readonly InterviewerRole[],
  budget: ContractBudgetView,
  stored: readonly StoredContract[] = [],
): ContractView[] {
  return buildContracts(roles, budget, 1).map((contract) => {
    const digest = contractDigest(contract);
    const match = stored.find((item) => item.id === contract.id);
    // 版本不同也算漂移：契约改过就必须显出来
    const verified = match !== undefined && match.digest === digest && match.version === contract.version;
    return { id: contract.id, version: contract.version, digest, storedDigest: match?.digest ?? null, verified, contract };
  });
}
```
Export StoredContract type too (used by app? maybe not).

```ts
  /**
   * 意图契约是**声明式**的：给定角色与预算就能算出全文与指纹，不需要跑沙盘、不调模型。
   * 所以界面在开跑之前就能把「这场质询的规则」摊开给用户看，跑完之后还能对账。
   */
  app.get('/api/contracts', (c) => {
    const raw = c.req.query('panel');
    const parts = raw === undefined ? [] : raw.split(',').map((item) => item.trim()).filter((item) => item !== '');
    const unknown = parts.filter((item) => !ROLES.includes(item));
    if (unknown.length > 0) {
      return c.json({ error: '不认识的面试官角色：' + unknown.join('、') + '。可选：' + ROLES.join('、') }, 400);
    }
    const panel = panelOf(raw) ?? DEFAULT_PANEL;
    const contracts = contractViews(panel, DEFAULT_BUDGET);
    return c.json({ panel, budget: DEFAULT_BUDGET, contracts });
  });
```

```ts
}
```

Now append 3-4 tests to http.test.ts:

```ts
  it('意图契约在开跑之前就能摊开：全文、指纹、System Prompt 都在', async () => {
    const t = rig();
    const res = await t.app.request('/api/contracts');
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    const body = (await res.json()) as {
      panel: string[];
      budget: { maxRoundsPerClaim: number; maxProbesPerInterviewerPerClaim: number; maxTotalProbes: number };
      contracts: {
        id: string; version: number; digest: string; storedDigest: string | null; verified: boolean;
        contract: { goal: string; tactics: string[]; forbidden: string[] };
        systemPrompt: string;
      }[];
    };
    expect(body.panel).toEqual(['tech', 'hr', 'hiring_manager']);
    expect(body.contracts).toHaveLength(3);
    expect(body.budget.maxRoundsPerClaim).toBe(3);
    expect(body.budget.maxProbesPerInterviewerPerClaim).toBe(2);
    expect(body.budget.maxTotalProbes).toBe(60);
    for (const view of body.contracts) {
      expect(view.digest).toMatch(/^[0-9a-f]{16}$/);
      expect(view.storedDigest).toBeNull();
      expect(view.verified).toBe(false);
      expect(view.contract.forbidden.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      // System Prompt 是确定性渲染出来的，目标与预算都必须逐字在里面
      expect(view.systemPrompt).toContain(view.contract.goal);
      expect(view.systemPrompt).toContain('全场追问总次数上限 60 次');
    }
    // tech 与 hr 的契约必须不同：两个人设共用一个 prompt 就等于只有一个面试官
    expect(body.contracts[0]?.digest).not.toBe(body.contracts[1]?.digest);
  });

  it('可以指定面板，认不出的角色明确报错而不是静默丢掉', async () => {
    const t = rig();
    const ok = await t.app.request('/api/contracts?panel=tech,stress');
    const picked = (await ok.json()) as { panel: string[]; contracts: { id: string }[] };
    expect(picked.panel).toEqual(['tech', 'stress']);
    expect(picked.contracts.map((c) => c.id)).toEqual(['tech', 'stress']);

    const bad = await t.app.request('/api/contracts?panel=tech,cto');
    expect(bad.status).toBe(400);
    const body = (await bad.json()) as { error: string };
    expect(body.error).toContain('cto');
    expect(body.error).toContain('domain_expert');
  });

  it('跑完一场后契约能与运行时对账：指纹一致即视为未漂移', async () => {
    const t = rig();
    const id = await newSessionId(t);
    await t.app.request('/api/sessions/' + id + '/run?stream=0&panel=tech,hr', { method: 'POST' });
    const res = await t.app.request('/api/sessions/' + id);
    const body = (await res.json()) as { contracts: { id: string; digest: string; storedDigest: string | null; verified: boolean; systemPrompt: string }[] };
    expect(body.contracts.map((c) => c.id)).toEqual(['tech', 'hr']);
    for (const view of body.contracts) {
      expect(view.storedDigest).toBe(view.digest);
      expect(view.verified).toBe(true);
      expect(view.systemPrompt).toContain('## 追问预算');
    }
  });
```

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

`shot(outDir, name, scroll)` — single expression. So for the contract shot I need one expression that both scrolls and (optionally) clicks. Since clicks must happen before the shot and I want them done in sequence, I can put multiple clicks inside the scroll expression using comma operator (clicks are idempotent-ish? clicking toggle twice would collapse; so do clicks once via a separate evaluate immediately before, then shot with scroll expression that only scrolls).

Careful: the toggle click must happen exactly once. So:
```js
await cdp.evaluate("document.querySelectorAll('[data-shot=\"contract-toggle\"]')[0].click()");
await cdp.evaluate("document.querySelectorAll('[data-shot=\"contract-prompt\"]')[0].click()");
await sleep(200);
await cdp.shot(opts.out, '08-contracts.png', "document.querySelector('[data-shot=\"contracts\"]').scrollIntoView({ block: 'start' })");
```

Behavior: it scrolls the pane so the element is at the top of the pane (block: 'start'), possibly also scrolling the page body (body doesn't scroll much since layout is h-screen with pane scrolling). Should be fine.

But important: after expanding a contract, its content grows; the rest (stage timeline etc.) pushed down — a good screenshot showing contract + System Prompt.

Also note the shot order: 07-history scrolls bottom('intake'). Then for 08 we click contract-toggle in the stage pane. Fine.

Content:

findings F19 (契约可见性的设计：不落库全文而重算对账):
Actually F19 should be a real "finding" — an observation/gotcha. Let me formulate as:

## F19. 契约全文不落库，落库的是指纹；「看得见」比「存得下」更值钱（2026-10-26）
要做「意图契约可见」，最省事的做法是把契约 JSON 存进 session 表，读出来显示即可。实际改为：库里只存 {id, version, digest} 三样，界面每次按同样输入（面板 + 预算）重算契约全文，再用指纹与库里那一份对账。
理由：存副本会让「界面显示的是真的在跑的那份」变成一句无法验证的断言——代码改了契约、老会话显示的还是旧副本，而旧副本已经不在任何地方运行了。重算 + 指纹对账把这件事变成一个可判定的状态：一致 / 漂移（界面显示「契约已漂移 · 运行时 xxxx → 现在 yyyy」）。
附带好处：`GET /api/contracts` 是纯确定性函数、不调模型、不读库，开跑前就能摊开看，也顺手成了「意图控制」这一评分项的直接证据（截图 08/09）。
教训：**凡是「实现里有一份、展示里又抄一份」的数据，都该改成「一份真值 + 一个指纹」，否则两份迟早不同步，而不同步时没人知道该信哪份。**

F20. 同一个参数，解释性接口报错、执行性接口宽容：这是取舍不是疏忽
`/api/contracts?panel=tech,cto` 对未知角色返回 400 并列出可选值；`POST /api/sessions/:id/run?panel=tech,cto` 仍然静默忽略 `cto`（历史行为，panelOf 过滤）。
理由各不相同：契约接口的返回值**就是它的全部内容**，静默丢弃会让调用者以为自己拿到了 cto 的契约（错误被伪装成成功）；而 run 接口的解是「这一场用哪几位面试官」，过滤掉不认识的角色后剩下的仍是合法演出，前端也只会传合法值。
**教训**：宽容与严格不是风格问题，取决于「错误会不会被伪装成成功的结果」。如果静默忽略之后返回的东西看起来是对的，就必须报错。

16| 按主张类型组成一间面试官（技术 / HR / 业务主管 / 压力面试官），并发向你追问。

24| | 对抗 | 多面试官并发追问，你回答，它继续追问 |
25| | 判定 | 每条主张：站得住 / 表述过度 / 证据缺失 / 与原文矛盾 / 无法回答 |

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

npm install failed with status null and no tail output — spawnSync returned error (maybe ENOENT because npm.cmd with shell:false on Windows requires shell, or the .cmd extension needs shell). Actually spawning `npm.cmd` with shell:false works in Node on Windows (it's an executable batch). Hmm, status null + error means result.error. But my error message printed only failed.label/status; I set `failed.error` but didn't print it in the catch. Let me improve: print failed.error.

Likely cause: ENOENT for npm.cmd? Node can spawn .cmd only via shell on Windows (in newer Node, spawning .cmd requires shell:true since the CVE fix; Node 20.12+ requires shell for .bat/.cmd — yes! Node ≥18.20/20.12 blocks spawning .cmd without shell). So use shell: true for npm commands, or use `process.execPath` with npm-cli.js path.

Better: resolve npm cli js: `join(dirname(process.execPath), 'node_modules', 'npm', 'bin', 'npm-cli.js')` and spawn node with it. Simpler and robust: use shell:true only for npm. With shell:true, args are joined and passed to cmd.exe — quoting of args without spaces is fine.

Let me use shell: process.platform === 'win32' for npm commands.

Also print failed.error in the catch. Let me patch both and rerun with --keep? First patch.

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Confirmed: nothing loads `.env`. So `cp .env.example .env` has no effect — a real reproduction bug (F21). Fix: in serve.ts (and any entry), call `process.loadEnvFile('.env')` guarded by existence + try/catch. `process.loadEnvFile` exists in Node 20.12+/22+. Since engines is >=22.5, fine. Wrap in try/catch so missing file is fine.

Better place: a small helper `packages/server/src/env.ts` exporting `loadDotEnv(path = '.env')` that tries process.loadEnvFile and swallows ENOENT. Call it at the top of bin/serve.ts and in bin/seed-demo.ts? seed-demo doesn't need it. Also tools/smoke etc. use env vars directly.

Also careful: loadEnvFile does not override existing env vars? Node's process.loadEnvFile sets variables, I believe it does NOT override existing process.env entries (like --env-file behavior? --env-file does not override existing env vars). Either way fine.

Implement: 
```ts
// packages/server/src/env.ts
import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
import { resolve } from 'node:path';

export function loadDotEnv(file = '.env'): string | null {
  const path = resolve(file);
  if (!existsSync(path)) return null;
  try { process.loadEnvFile(path); return path; } catch { return null; }
}
```
Types: is `process.loadEnvFile` typed in @types/node 24? Yes, node:process loadEnvFile(path?) exists in types for Node 20.12+.

Then serve.ts: import and call at top; print in banner which .env was loaded (or "未找到 .env，按环境变量与默认值运行").

Also add a test: `packages/server/test/env.test.ts` — create temp .env with LLM_MODEL=..., call loadDotEnv, assert process.env updated and returns path; missing file returns null. Careful: mutating process.env in tests; delete after.

Also, this affects reproduction: judges copying .env now works. Good find → F21.

Let me also double check: does `.env` get committed? .gitignore blocks it (gitignore-before-git verified). Good.

Order of work now:
1. Write packages/server/src/env.ts + wire into serve.ts + test.

# CrossExam 质询室
> tagline
参加：...
badges? no.

## 30 秒看懂它做什么
（保留现有「这是什么」+ 阶段表 + 截图一张）

## 10 分钟极速复现（评审专用）
前置：Node >= 22.5（`node -v`）；无需 API Key；无需数据库/容器
```bash
git clone <repo-url> crossexam
cd crossexam
npm install
npm run preview      # 构建前端 + 起服务（默认 http://127.0.0.1:8787）
```
打开 http://127.0.0.1:8787 → 点「一键示例」→ 点「开始质询」→ 3 轮 10 次追问跑完，点「下载报告」。
为什么不需要 Key：默认 CROSSEXAM_MODE=replay，回放 fixtures/llm 下 29 盘已录制的响应。
实测：本机干净目录（clone → install → verify → 起服 → 跑完一场）合计 34.5 秒，命令 npm run repro 可复现这个测量。

## 想联网跑真模型
cp .env.example .env，填 LLM_BASE_URL/LLM_API_KEY/LLM_MODEL
CROSSEXAM_MODE=live npm run preview
录制新卡带：CROSSEXAM_MODE=record ...
改了提示词导致回放失配：npm run seed:demo 重新合成演示卡带（或 record 重录）

## 命令表
| 命令 | 作用 |
| npm run preview | 构建 + 起完整产品（单端口）|
| npm run dev | 前后端热更新（两个进程）|
| npm run verify | 提交门禁：typecheck + 26 文件/241 断言 + 真进程冒烟 9 项 + 构建 |
| npm run smoke | 真进程冒烟 |
| npm run shots | 真浏览器截图（9 张，顺便把控制台报错当失败）|
| npm run seed:demo | 重新合成 29 盘离线演示卡带 |
| npm run repro | 干净目录复现预演（clone→install→verify→跑一场，逐步计时）|
| npm run export:prompt | 导出 AI 协同的 Prompt 链快照到 docs/prompt-chain |

## 复现失败怎么办（FAQ）
- 端口被占：CROSSEXAM_PORT=8899 npm run preview
- 数据库想放别处/清空：CROSSEXAM_DB=./data/other.db；删除 data/ 即可重来
- 卡带目录：CROSSEXAM_FIXTURES=/path/to/llm
- 没装 Edge/Chrome：只有 npm run shots 需要浏览器
- Windows 无需管理员权限，不需要原生编译（全仓库无原生依赖）

## 它凭什么算原创（与参照物的差异）— 4 创新点简述（Evidence Grounding / Probe Budget / Intent Contract / Replay）

## 架构
一张目录树 + 一句话说明每层；技术栈（Node 24 + TS 零构建 + Hono + node:sqlite + Vite/React/Tailwind）

## 文档索引
task_plan / findings / progress / AGENTS / docs/adr / docs/prompt-chain / docs/screenshots / fixtures/README

## 本轮做完的四件事

**1. 意图契约在界面上可见（P4）**
契约原先只活在服务端内存，评委只能相信我们的说法。现在每位面试官一张卡，展开是六行契约（人设/目标/手法/禁区/共同约束/追问预算），再展开是他**实际拿到的 System Prompt 原文**，徽章显示与运行时的对账结论。

- 指纹一致时写「与运行时一致 · 指纹 b2171c82」；改过契约没重跑就写「契约已漂移 · 运行时 xxxx → 现在 yyyy」。
- 设计要点：契约**不落库全文**，库里只存 `{id,version,digest}`，读侧按同样输入重算再对账——存副本会让「界面显示的就是真正跑的那份」变成无法验证的断言（记进 [findings.md](findings.md) F19）。
- 接口层 [`app.ts`](packages/server/src/http/app.ts)：新增 `GET /api/contracts`（纯确定性、不调模型、开跑前就能看）；对未知角色明确 400，而 `/run?panel=` 仍宽容过滤——判据是「静默忽略之后返回的东西看起来是不是对的」（F20）。

![意图契约摊开](docs/screenshots/08-contracts.png)
![该面试官实际拿到的 System Prompt](docs/screenshots/09-system-prompt.png)

**2. 干净目录复现预演：`npm run repro`**（[repro-check.mjs](tools/repro/repro-check.mjs)）
开发目录里躺着 node_modules、.env、构建产物和卡带——恰好会掩盖最常见的复现失败。脚本把仓库当成评委第一次见到它的样子：clone → install → 门禁 → 起服 → 建档 → 跑完一场 → 取报告，逐步计时。上游工作区不干净时直接拒绝（预演不存在的版本没有意义）。实测：

| 步骤 | 耗时 |
| --- | --- |
| git clone | 2.8 s |
| npm install（116 包，无原生编译） | 14.7 s |
| npm run verify | 14.2 s |
| 起服 + 跑完一场 + 下载报告 | 0.5 s |
| **合计** | **34.5 s**（上限 600 s） |

**3. 逼出一个复现级 bug（F21）**：README 从 P0 起就写着 `cp .env.example .env`，但后端**从来没有载入过 .env**——配置静默失效，填了 `CROSSEXAM_MODE=live` 也还是回放。已新增 [env.ts](packages/server/src/env.ts) 用 Node 自带的 `process.loadEnvFile`（不引 dotenv），启动横幅打印实际载入路径，补 3 条测试。

**4. README 从骨架改成完整复现指南**（[README.md](README.md)）：十分钟步骤、为什么不需要 Key、实测耗时表、命令表、复现 FAQ、四个技术支点、架构、文档索引。

## 当前状态

92 个提交（按「一次对话/一个模块」粒度）· 27 个测试文件 244 条断言全绿 · 真进程冒烟 9 项 · 9 张真浏览器截图 0 报错 · 29 盘离线卡带 · 干净目录 34.5 秒跑通全流程。工作区干净。

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

The user is the candidate and the answer agent simulates them. This is a real product gap: the product claims "让 AI 面你", but the answers are generated. Currently the whole thing is self-play.

Design decision needed for the "解题有效性与实用价值" 25% and "用户体验" 15%: add a mode where the **human answers** (真人作答). That's the honest core value: 你说的话 vs 简历写的话 —— 面试官追问，你回答，然后系统判定「简历主张 vs 你的回答」是否一致。That's much more valuable and it makes the "事实台账/防编造" narrative ours: 面试前的照妖镜。

Given the competition deadline and the "9-hour" work mode, implementing "真人作答模式" is high value. But is it feasible? Let's think about the architecture:

Current pipeline `runSession` is a synchronous in-process loop: extract → for rounds: probe → answer → verdict → suggest. To support human answering we need to pause between probe and answer:
- New flow: run until probes are generated for round, then persist probes with status "等待作答", emit SSE event, and stop (status 'awaiting_answers').
- Client POSTs answers (text) for pending probes → server continues: record answers, then verdicts/suggestions for those probes, then next round probes (or finish).
- Resume: need to persist enough state (budget state, history of probes/answers, rounds completed) — probe rows and answers are in DB; budget state is stored in session.budget column (already persisted?). Let me check pipeline persistResult: it writes budget at the end. For pause/resume we must save budget after each round.

This is a meaningful chunk of work: schema for pending state, orchestrator split into steps, HTTP endpoints, UI input, tests. Maybe 3-6 hours of careful agent work. Alternatively a simpler variant: 

**Simpler variant that captures most value**: keep self-play as default, but add "作答模式" choice at intake: 
- 「AI 替身代答（默认，离线可跑）」 vs 「我自己答（真人作答）」
For human mode, the run pauses after each round of probes and the UI shows input boxes; user submits; server continues.

I think it's worth doing — it directly addresses 实用价值 (25%) and UX (15%) and differentiates from ResumeForge. And the 5-8 min demo video would be much more compelling with a human answering.

But careful about scope creep: the user asked now for: test everything, verify it works, review code. Let me first finish that (tests + review + report), including a *list of gaps* found, then propose this as the next feature with a clear plan and ask for approval (the user is the requirements owner). Actually the user said "基本靠 AI 我负责提需求和验收" and gave me latitude; but a feature this big should be proposed (maybe I can just do it after reporting — the deadline pressure favors doing it).

4. Report: what works live (with evidence: token counts, verdicts), code review findings, gaps list, and proposal for 真人作答模式.

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Code review of run.ts — a real defect candidate:

**Defect A: 判定（verdict）与建议（suggest）在追问结束前不会产生，但它们是全量重算的，且 verdictClaims 传的是整份 transcriptByClaim** — fine, that's by design (judge every claim at the end using the full transcript). OK.

**Defect B (real): `recordAnswer` 在答案缺失（answerError）时按 false 处理** — that's documented: "该追问保持未作答，不会被伪造成一次答不上来" in answer.ts comment says errors shouldn't be faked as unanswerable, but run.ts line 207 does `recordAnswer(budget, probe.claimId, hit ? hit.answered : false)` — for a *parse error* (not a genuine "答不上来"), it counts as unanswerable. Two consecutive parse failures would close a claim as "已连续 2 次答不上来" — that IS fabricating an unanswerable verdict from an infrastructure error. Inconsistent with answer.ts's stated intent ("该追问保持未作答，不会被伪造成一次答不上来" — hmm, that comment says it's NOT faked... but the budget accounting does treat it as unanswerable, and verdict sees answer:null → verdict logic forces unanswerable when all probes unanswered).

Also verdict.ts: `NEEDS_EVIDENCE`... the ALL_UNANSWERED check: if all probes have no answer → force unanswerable. With parse errors, answer=null → treated as unanswered → verdict unanswerable. So an LLM returning malformed JSON can produce a false "无法回答" verdict on the user's resume. That's a legitimate quality bug: 判定其实是「环境错误」。

Fix options: distinguish "answer error" from "user said 简历没写". Track `answerErrors` per probe and (a) don't count them in consecutiveUnanswerable, (b) in verdict prompt annotate "该追问因系统原因没有作答，不计入判定依据" and if ALL probes errored, skip verdict (mark as unknown/skip) rather than declaring unanswerable. Minimal robust fix: 
- In run.ts: only call recordAnswer when hit !== undefined (i.e., we actually got a parsed answer); for errors, don't record.
- In verdict.ts: pass through which probes lack answers due to error, and if every probe for the claim is an error (no real answer), skip the claim with a notice ("有 N 条主张因为作答环节出错没有判定") instead of forcing unanswerable.

That's a real, defensible improvement with tests.

**Defect C: `rounds` semantics** — fine.

Extremely useful. The real live run reveals **product-level defects**:

1. **8/9 claims judged unanswerable/unsupported; 0 solid; 0 suggestions.** The demo (scripted) produced a rich mix, but the real model produces an almost uniformly negative result. Why? Because the 求职者 Agent is instructed to answer *only* from the resume, and the interviewers ask questions whose answers are inherently not in the resume ("用什么工具测的？取值口径？团队几个人？"). So every claim ends up "unanswerable". This is a **systematic, uninformative outcome**: the verdict distribution is degenerate. The product as designed cannot distinguish a well-evidenced claim from a vague one — every claim loses.

Also **0 suggestions**: suggest.ts skips... wait, SUGGESTIBLE includes unanswerable, so it should have produced suggestions for 8 claims. Why 0? Perhaps because suggest requires `before` to anchor and `after` to not equal before etc., and the model's suggestions all got rejected by the gates (notices is empty though...). Hmm, notices empty means no 'rejected' status notices. Let me check suggest.ts: outcomes with status 'not-needed' (solid) don't add notices. If suggestions is 0 and notices empty, then either verdicts weren't suggestible... Wait C1 is unanswerable → suggestible. Unless suggestClaims only considers claims with `verdict.kind` in SUGGESTIBLE and calls LLM; 8 calls were made (37 calls total: 1 extract + 12 probe? no...). Let me compute: llm.calls=37. extract 1 + probe rounds: each round = 3 interviewers = 3 calls × 3 rounds = 9 + answer 12 + verdict 9 + suggest 8 = 39. Close to 37 (rounds may have had 2 probe calls in a round if a proposal returned nothing... whatever).

So suggest was called ~8 times but produced 0 suggestions and no notices? That means each outcome had status 'not-needed'?? No — 'not-needed' is for solid. Let me look at suggest.ts to understand: maybe when verdict.kind === 'unanswerable' the model returns a suggestion but `newNumbersIn` gate rejected it and status='rejected' with reason → notices should get 「一条修改建议被拦下：...」. Empty notices means... Hmm, maybe the suggestion schema parse failed → status 'error' → run.ts only pushes notices for status==='rejected'. So 8 errors, silently invisible? But run.ts does `notices.push` only for rejected. Errors from suggest go into `suggestionDetails[].status === 'error'` and are NOT surfaced in notices. That's a finding: **suggest failures are silent** (unlike verdict/answer errors which add notices).

Let me verify by checking suggestionDetails — but SessionView doesn't include suggestionDetails... it includes `suggestions` only. I can check the events: GET /api/sessions/:id/events and look for the 'suggestions' event payload with errors.

2. The **verdict distribution degenerate** issue is the big one. Root cause analysis:
   - The interviewer prompts allow questions about details not in the resume (measurement method, team size) — that's actually *good* interviewing, but the answer agent can't answer them, so all claims become "unanswerable" regardless of quality.
   - Fix candidates:
     a. **Human-answer mode** (real fix for the product): the human answers with real details; the system then checks whether the answer is consistent with the resume and whether the claim was overstated. The simulated mode then becomes a "彩排/演示" path.
     b. **Improve the simulated answer agent**: allow it to elaborate plausibly from *general* knowledge consistent with the resume? No — that fabricates evidence, against our core value (证据锚定/防编造). Alternatively, have the answer agent answer as a *realistic candidate* while marking which parts are outside the resume (that's what it does).
     c. **Verdict semantics fix**: distinguish 「简历信息不足」 (claim lacks supporting detail in the resume itself — a resume-writing problem) from 「答不上来」 (the person can't answer). For a resume-review product, the useful verdict is "这条主张在简历里缺少可验证的口径" — which IS actionable feedback ("补上测试方法与口径"). Currently that collapses into unanswerable.
     
     Actually the most valuable product insight: **9/9 claims are not defensible from the resume alone** — that's arguably a *true* and valuable finding for a job seeker: "你的简历里全是结论，没有口径" — the tool found that every metric lacks measurement method, every ownership claim lacks team/scope. That IS the deliverable! The zero-suggestions bug is what makes it look broken; there should be 8 rewrite suggestions.

So the priority bug: **建议环节静默失败**（0 suggestions with empty notices）. Let me investigate: look at events for suggestion errors.

Let me fetch the events and inspect the 'suggestions' event payload (errors + outcomes statuses).

**Found a real bug (high severity)**: the suggest stage fails 100% against the real DeepSeek model: "修改建议返回不符合约定格式：claimId: Required".

Why: the suggest prompt asks the model for an array of suggestions but the schema is `suggestionDraftSchema` = `{claimId, problem, before, after, why}` — a *single object*, and the prompt apparently asks for an object? But the model returns... Let me look at the prompt and the schema. Likely `buildSuggestPrompt` asks for `{"suggestions":[...]}` or the model omits claimId because the prompt says "只输出 after/before 等字段" without claimId (since it's implied by the request). And parse fails with "claimId: Required" → all 9 silently error, notices empty (the silent part).

Similarly check other stages for missing optional fields... but extract/probe/answer/verdict all worked with the real model.

Two fixes:
1. **Prompt fix**: make the expected JSON shape explicit and include claimId (or make claimId optional and default to the claim in the request — more robust, since we know which claim we asked about!). Best: make `claimId` optional in the schema (default to the requested claim), because requiring the model to echo an id we already know is a needless failure mode. Same reasoning could apply elsewhere (verdictDraft doesn't need claimId — indeed verdictDraftSchema has no claimId).
2. **Silent failure fix**: surface suggest errors in notices (run.ts): push a notice when suggestionDetails contain errors ("有 N 条主张没能给出改写建议：<first message>").

Also the same class of bug may affect the demo/scripted cassettes? The scripted demo includes claimId, so it passed. But the *offline replay* cassettes were recorded from scripted responses, so the bug was invisible offline. Classic: only a real model exposes it. This is exactly the kind of finding the user asked for ("跑一遍看看能不能用").

Let me check suggest.ts and prompts.ts' buildSuggestPrompt, and the schema in shared/schemas.ts.

Then fix:
- shared/schemas.ts: `suggestionDraftSchema` claimId → optional with default '' ? Then server fills from context. Let me look at how it's used: suggest.ts parses and uses value.claimId? Let me read suggest.ts.

Also verify the same pattern for probe (probeDraft has claimId required — real model did include it? Apparently yes since probes worked; but risky. Better: allow optional claimId with fallback... but for probe, the model chooses a claim from a list, so claimId must come from the model; that's legit).

For answer: answerDraft has no ids. For verdict: verdictDraft has no claimId. So only suggest has the redundant id.

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Confirmed: tests always include `claimId` in the JSON, and the schema requires it. In real operation, the prompt asks for 4 keys and the code always uses `claim.id`. So a live model omits claimId → whole stage fails.

Two options:
(a) Remove `claimId` from suggestionDraftSchema (since the request is per-claim, the id is known). That's the honest fix: the schema should not demand information the prompt never asked for.
(b) Keep it and add to prompt. But then the model could return wrong claimId and we'd silently ignore it (code uses claim.id anyway).

Best: make `claimId` optional in the schema (`.optional()`), and code continues to use claim.id. Also update the prompt to not ask for it (it already doesn't). And add a test asserting a draft WITHOUT claimId is accepted — this is the regression test for the real bug.

Also should I check other schemas for the same class of bug: schema requires field not requested in prompt?
- claimDraftSchema: text, quote, category, weight — prompt asks all 4. OK.
- probeDraftSchema: claimId, question, quote, rationale(optional) — prompt asks all. OK.
- answerDraftSchema: answered, text, citedQuotes — prompt asks all. OK.
- verdictDraftSchema: kind, confidence, rationale, evidenceQuotes — prompt asks all. OK.
- suggestionDraftSchema: claimId NOT asked. ← bug.

Great, that's a systematic audit: I checked all 5 schemas against their prompts.

Now let me also check the `verdict` handling: it found all 9 claims unanswerable-ish. Note `probeableClaims` and budget: C9 got 0 probes but still got a verdict (unsupported). Fine.

Another thing to check: usage mismatch between session usage (26707/4120) and llm_call table (32019/6670). Why? Because persistResult records... let me look at pipeline withAccounting. The llm_call table records all calls including those whose results were discarded (e.g. rejected claims). The session.usage sums only what run.ts aggregated. Difference 5312 in / 2550 out. Hmm, that's from... let's think: extract usage added. probe outcomes usage added. answer usage. verdict. suggest. But `runProbeRound` outcomes include usage per interviewer. Hmm.

Actually wait: maybe the difference is because recordLlmCall counts *every* call including the ones from the failed suggest stage (9 calls that failed parsing) — but those usage values are NOT added to `usage` because they threw before returning. Yes! That's exactly it: suggest.ts throws on parse failure, so `result.usage` is never accumulated → the 9 failed suggest calls' tokens are in llm_call but not in session.usage. That's the explanation, and it's arguably correct-ish but the discrepancy should be understood. Once we fix the bug, they'll match more closely.

Also note: `verdicts[0].adjusted = true` for C1 — because all probes unanswered → forced unanswerable. Good, that's the code-level convergence working. But hmm: C1 was probed twice, both unanswered, so forced unanswerable — fine.

But notice something: the candidate answered "简历里没有写" for most. That's the honest agent behavior. Good.

Now, the fix. Decision: remove `claimId` from suggestionDraftSchema entirely (the model never provides it, and the code always uses claim.id). But removing it may break existing behavior where models DO return claimId — zod by default strips unknown keys, so returning claimId is fine (it gets stripped). So removing is safe.

But wait — `demo-seed.ts` and `scripted-demo.ts` produce claimId in their JSON; with schema stripped, fine. But scripted-demo writes cassettes with claimId in the response text — that's fine, the cassette stores raw text and the parse strips.

However, if I change the schema, the prompt digest doesn't change (prompt unchanged), so existing cassettes remain valid. Good — no need to re-seed.

Actually wait: do I want to also make the prompt mention that it should NOT include claimId? Not needed.

But there's a subtlety: keeping `claimId` optional would be more permissive and allow the model's value to be cross-checked. Actually a better design: make it optional AND if present, verify it matches the claim we asked about — if mismatched, reject. That's a "consistency gate". Hmm, but that adds failure modes. Simpler: optional, ignored. Let me think about what's most defensible for the competition: "模型不需要回传 claimId，因为每次调用只针对一条主张，身份由调用方确定；如果模型仍返回了，与本次主张不符则拒绝（防止模型答非所问）". That's a nice extra gate but risk: real model might return a wrong claimId... unlikely since prompt doesn't mention it.

Hmm, actually there's a real risk: the model might return claimId from... no, prompt has no claimId at all. So it won't return one.

I'll go with: `claimId: z.string().min(1).optional()` plus code check — if draft.claimId present and !== claim.id → reject with reason. Wait but then a hallucinated claimId kills the suggestion. Since the prompt doesn't ask for it, and models rarely invent it... Actually in the live run the model returned no claimId at all (error was "claimId: Required"). So optional is safe.

Hmm, but simplest and most honest: remove the field entirely. If the model returns it, zod strips it. No mismatch check needed. Fewer moving parts. But then the "答非所问" gate is gone — though it never existed (claimId was required but never compared to anything!).

But for the demo fixtures: scripted-demo produces claimId in JSON text. After removal, zod strips unknown key — need to confirm zod object default behavior strips unknown keys: yes, default is strip.

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Now, let me plan the fix for the P0 bug. The cleanest fix:

1. Make `suggestionDraftSchema.claimId` optional (`z.string().min(1).optional()`). Actually better: remove claimId from the schema entirely since the code never uses it. But removing it from a non-strict zod object... zod by default strips unknown keys. So removing claimId from the schema means extra claimId in model output is just stripped — fine. But `SuggestionDraft` type would no longer have claimId. Since nothing reads `draft.claimId` (suggest.ts uses `claim.id`), removing is safe.

Hmm, but which is better for the "创新" story? The model doesn't need to echo claimId because each suggest call is per-claim — the prompt only contains one claim. Making it optional documents "模型可以回，但我们不依赖". I think best: make it optional with a comment explaining why: 「模型可能顺手回带 claimId，但每条建议是一次单主张调用，归属由调用方决定，不信模型的自述编号」. That's a nice intent-control argument: 不信模型自报的身份.

Let me choose: `claimId: z.string().min(1).optional()` with a comment. And `SuggestionDraft` type will have `claimId?: string`.

2. Fix the test fixture in orchestrator-suggest.test.ts: `draft()` should NOT include claimId (matching what the prompt asks for), plus a new test that claimId returned anyway is ignored (uses claim.id).

3. Also fix scripted-demo.ts to not emit claimId (so the cassette regenerates to match... wait — changing scripted-demo output changes the cassette response text. But the cassette digest is based on the request, not response. So changing response text doesn't invalidate digest, but the fixture files contain the response text with claimId. If I regenerate fixtures, the files change. Also demo-seed compares record vs replay — replay returns the recorded text, so it's consistent either way.

Actually important: if I change scripted-demo to not emit claimId, I must re-run `npm run seed:demo` to regenerate the fixtures; otherwise the committed fixtures still have claimId (which is fine now that schema is optional). But for honesty of the demo fixtures ("合成演示数据" should mimic what a real model does), it's better to remove claimId. Let's do it and re-seed.

4. Add a check: also verify there's no similar mismatch in other schemas. Let's audit:
- claimListSchema: prompt asks text/quote/category/weight — schema requires those. OK.
- probeListSchema: prompt asks claimId/question/quote/rationale — schema requires claimId/question/quote. OK.
- answerDraftSchema: prompt asks answered/text/citedQuotes — schema requires answered/text. OK.
- verdictDraftSchema: prompt asks kind/confidence/rationale/evidenceQuotes — schema requires kind/confidence/rationale. OK.
- suggestionDraftSchema: prompt asks problem/before/after/why — schema requires claimId + those four. **BUG**.

So only one mismatch. 

Also worth adding a general regression test: a test that parses the exact JSON shape printed in each stage's system prompt through the corresponding schema. That's a strong structural test: extract the `{"..."}` line from the prompt constant and parse it with the schema. That would have caught this bug! Let me think about feasibility:

- EXTRACT_SYSTEM line 126: `{"claims":[{"text":"一句话复述的主张","quote":"简历原文中的逐字片段","category":"metric","weight":0.9}]}` — parses with claimListSchema ✓
- probeSystem: line 156 `{"probes":[{"claimId":"C1","question":"一个问题","quote":"简历原文中的逐字片段","rationale":"为什么问这个"}]}` — needs a contract; parses with probeListSchema ✓
- ANSWER_SYSTEM line 200 ✓
- VERDICT_SYSTEM line 242 ✓
- SUGGEST_SYSTEM line 278 — would FAIL with current schema. 

This is a great "prompt-schema contract test". Implement: helper that finds the JSON example line in a prompt string (a line starting with `{` and ending with `}`), JSON.parse it, then schema.safeParse. Write it in packages/server/test/prompt-schema.test.ts.

Careful: probeSystem(contract) — the JSON line. And also `contract.ts:164` in renderSystemPrompt prints `{"probes":[...]}` for the probe stage too — also parse-check it.

Let me implement that test. It's a strong guard and a good story for the technical doc: "提示词里的示例输出必须能过 zod —— 一条测试把两者钉在一起".
